       # for all l, c += -v[idx+l] + h[idx-l]

       h[idx]=1

Binary local search function:
`binary_refine(h, rng, budget_s, time_limit, target)`:
- Given binary h (sum n/2). Maintain c array (full correlate via np.correlate or compute from h). max_c = max(c), best_c = max(c)*2/n.
- Use current h and c copies. We'll perform improvement passes:
   * Hot-lag targeted swaps: For top K lags (say 8), consider up to M candidate swaps per lag. For positive lag L: indices i 0..n-1-L where h_i=1, h_{i+L}=0; swapping i->0, j=i+L->1. Let lag_value = idx-(n-1). If lag_value>0, pairs (i, i+lag_value). If negative, lag_value=-L, pairs (i, i-L) with h_i=1,h_{i-L}=0 (swap i to0, i-L to1). We can find indices using nonzero arrays:
      if L>0: ones=where h[:n-L]==1 and h[L:]==0; candidates (i, i+L).
      if L<0: L=-lag; candidates (i+L, i) where h[L:]==1 and h[:n-L]==0? h_i=1, i-L=h_{i+L?}: for pair (i, i+L) with h_i? wait negative lag -L: c_{-L}=∑_{i=0}^{n-1-L} h_{i+L}(1-h_i). Pairs (i+L=1, i=0). Swap i (0)->1, i+L (1)->0. Candidate (one=i+L, zero=i).
   * Random swaps: pick random one and zero.
- For each candidate, compute D using original h bits? Since inside function h changes. For targeted sequence, use current h.
   `swap_delta(h, i, j)` returns D array length 2n-1. It can be computed O(n) using slices. Then new_max = max(c + D). But allocating D each candidate O(n) memory. For n=2048, okay. For many candidates, optimize by computing delta on arrays? Could precompute A_i/B_i? Not.
   Actually we can compute D as full array; use np.add with slices. D = np.empty? We can reuse a preallocated `delta` array and zero it each candidate (O(n)). Then update. For each candidate:
      delta.fill(0)
      v = 1 - h (since h bits). But h is int; v maybe int.

Better binary search on λ without pre clip:
```
def project_sum_box(y,target):
    lo = y.min() - 1.0; hi = y.max() + 1.0
    # f(lambda)=sum(clip(y-lambda,0,1))
    # f(lo)>=target? if lo=min(y)-1, y-lo >=1 for all? min+1-lo =1, so f=n >=target. hi max+1 -> f=0.
    for _ in range(50):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       val=np.clip(y-mid,0,1).sum()
       if val > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    lam=(lo+hi)/2
    return np.clip(y-lam,0,1)
```
This produces correct projection onto intersection of box and hyperplane? Is projection onto sum under L2 with box solved by a shift? Yes for box+sum, the Euclidean projection onto {0≤x≤1, sum=target} is of form clip(y-λ,0,1) for some λ because constraints are separable plus one sum; Lagrangian. Good.

Let's implement `smooth_gradient(h, tau)`:
```
def smooth_gradient(h, tau):
  n=len(h); c=compute_c_full_fft(h); maxc=c.max()
  if tau<=0:
    tol=max(1e-10, 1e-7*maxc)
    active=c >= maxc - tol
    p=active.astype(float); p/=p.sum()
  else:
    w=np.exp((c - maxc)/tau)
    p=w/w.sum()
  H=maxc + (tau*np.log(w.sum()) if tau>0 else 0.0)
  # grad
  ...
  return H, maxc, p, G
```
Could avoid storing p? Need for gradient. p may have many tiny; for tau small, p sparse. If tau=0 active.

Potential optimization: If p support small (e.g., number of nonzero > threshold?), direct gradient via active lags may be faster than FFT. Compute direct:
```
G=np.zeros(n)
for idx, weight in enumerate(p): if abs(weight)>eps:
   l=idx-m
   if l>=0:

def minimize_integrand(convex, lo, hi):
  gr = (sqrt(5)-1)/2
  x1=hi-gr*(hi-lo)
  x2=lo+gr*(hi-lo)
  f1=f(x1); f2=f(x2)
  for ...:
    if f1 < f2: hi=x2; x2=x1; f2=f1; x1=hi-gr*(hi-lo); f1=f(x1)
    else: lo=x1; x1=x2; f1=f2; x2=lo+gr*(hi-lo); f2=f(x2)
  return (lo+hi)/2
```

```
def c_full_custom(h):
  n=len(h); v=1-h; c=np.empty(2*n-1)
  for l in range(-n+1,n):
     if l>=0: c[n-1+l] = np.dot(h[:n-l], v[l:])
     else: L=-l; c[n-1+l] = np.dot(h[L:], v[:n-L])
  return c
```

Optimization of line search evaluation:
```
def pair_eval_update_max(c, a, q1, q2, d, vals):
   np.multiply(a,d,out=vals)
   vals += c
   if d != 0.0:
      dd=d*d
      if q1>=0: vals[q1]+=dd
      if q2>=0: vals[q2]+=dd
   return vals.max()
```
But `vals += c` each eval overwrites. Good.

if len(active_idx)==0: active_idx=[argmax]
w=1/len
S=np.zeros(n)
v=1-h
m=n-1
for idx in active_idx:
  l=idx-m
  if l>=0:
    S[:n-l] += w * v[l:]
    S[l:]   -= w * h[:n-l]
  else:
    L=-l
    S[:n-L] += w * v[L:]
    S[:n-L] -= w * h[L:]
return S
```

       improved=False

                curmax, accepted = pair_line_search_update(...)

```
def random_composition(total, parts, rng):
  parts=max(1,min(parts,total))
  lengths=np.ones(parts,int)
  rem=total-parts
  if rem>0:
    weights=np.ones(parts,float)
    adds=rng.multinomial(rem, weights/weights.sum())
    lengths += adds
  return lengths
```

Now, full algorithm run:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):

Projection:
```
def project_sum_box(y, target):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    # lo/hi
    lo = float(y.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(y.max()) + 1.0
    # 50 iterations maybe
    for _ in range(45):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       val = np.clip(y-mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
       if val > target:
          lo=mid
       else:
          hi=mid
    lam=(lo+hi)*0.5
    z=np.clip(y-lam,0.0,1.0)
    # fine adjust for floating
    diff=target-z.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-13:
       mask=(z>1e-10)&(z<1.0-1e-10)
       if mask.any(): z[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
       z=np.clip(z,0,1)
    return z
```

Now, final exact C:
```
def exact_c5(h):
    n=len(h)
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    dx=2.0/n
    corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr)*dx)
```

Implementation pair_refine:
```
def pair_refine(h, rng, deadline):
    n=len(h); target=n/2.0; L=2*n-1; m=n-1
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # maybe project if sum off
    if abs(h.sum()-target)>1e-8: h=project_sum_box(h,target)
    c=c_full_fft(h)
    v=1.0-h
    a=np.empty(L); vals=np.empty(L)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.max(); best_C=best_c*2.0/n
    no_imp=0; round_attempts=0; accepted_total=0
    full_mode = (n <= 200)
    while time.time() < deadline:
       if full_mode:
          improved=False
          for i in range(n):
            for j in range(i+1,n):
               if time.time() >= deadline: return best_h
               curmax_delta = pair_line_search_update(c,h,v,i,j,float(best_c? no current max), a, vals)
               # curmax should track current c max. We need pass curmax. Maintain cur_max = float(c.max())? We can pass current_max = cur_max.
```
Pair helper returns new_max (max after update) and delta. It uses current_max for accept threshold. But current_max should be max(c). We can maintain `curmax` variable. Initially c.max(). After acceptance, helper returns newmax; set curmax = newmax; best update. After rejection, c unchanged so curmax same. But if c drift due numerical maybe? okay.
```
           newmax, d = pair_line_search_update(c,h,v,i,j,curmax,a,vals)
           if d != 0.0:
              h[i] += d; h[j] -= d
              # If hi/lo exact, maybe tiny out; clip? If clip, C update? We'll not clip unless outside >1e-9.
              if h[i] < 0: h[i]=0 ; if >1...
              v=1-h
              curmax = newmax
              if curmax < best_c - 1e-12:
                 best_c=curmax; best_h=h.copy(); improved=True
                 accepted_total++
              if (accepted_total % 50)==0:
                 # recompute c to remove drift and curmax
                 c=c_full_fft(h); curmax=float(c.max()); if curmax < best_c: update
              no_imp=0
          if not improved:
              # perturb if time
              if time.time() < deadline:
                 h = perturb(h,...); c=c_full_fft(h); curmax=...
```
But `best_c` is max c sums; `best_C` not needed inside. Use best_c for comparisons. `pair_line_search_update` uses `curmax` as current max; if c drift, okay.
Important: In full sweep, after accepting a move, the same pair may be improved further? The sweep order won't revisit until next pass. Good.

Heuristic loop:
```
K_lags=6; R_per_lag=6; max_candidates=200
count_accepts_round=0
while time<deadline:
  if round_attempts%20==0 or not top_lags:
     top_idx=np.argpartition(c, -K_lags)[::-1]
  candidates=[]
  seen=set()
  for idx in top_idx: 
     l=idx-m
     if l>0:
       L=l; score=h[:n-L]*(1-h[L:])
       if score.max()>1e-12:
          if len(score)>R: part=np.argpartition(score, -R)[-R:]
          else part=np.argsort(score)[-R:][::-1]
          for p in part: candidates.append((int(p), int(p+L)))
     elif l<0:
       L=-l; score=h[L:]*(1-h[:n-L])
       part top; for p: candidates.append((int(p+L), int(p)))
     else:
       # random pairs maybe
  # add random pairs up to 50
  while len(candidates)<max_random:
    i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n)); if i!=j: add
  # iterate candidates
  for (i,j) in candidates:
     if seen contains minmax continue
     v=1-h (ensure)
     newmax,d=pair_line_search_update(...)
     if d !=0: update; round_attempts=0; break? maybe continue but top stale; break to recompute.
     else: round_attempts+=1
```

def round_to_binary(h, rng=None):
  n=len(h); k=n//2
  if h.size != k*2: k=int(round(h.sum()))? But target n/2.
  if rng is None:
    order=np.argsort(-h, kind='mergesort')
  else:
    # random tie break: add tiny noise if values close? Simpler top with random permutation within equal? Use order = np.lexsort? 
    order=np.argsort(h + 1e-9*rng.random(n), kind='mergesort')[::-1]
  b=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int8); b[order[:k]]=1
  return b
```

Upsample:
```
def upsample_to(h_old, n_new):
  n_old=len(h_old)
  if n_old==n_new: return h_old.copy()
  x_old=np.linspace(0,2,n_old,endpoint=False)
  x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n_new,endpoint=False)
  return np.interp(x_new,x_old,h_old)
```

Now, review pair_line_search_update code in detail with arrays.
```
def pair_eval(c, a, q1, q2, d, vals):
    np.multiply(a, d, out=vals)
    vals += c
    if d != 0.0:
       dd = d*d
       if q1 >= 0:
          vals[q1] += dd
       if q2 >= 0:
          vals[q2] += dd
    return float(vals.max())
```
If q1==q2? not unless i=j. If q1 and q2 distinct. If q1 or q2 maybe out of range? Ldiff between -(n-1) and n-1, m=Ldiff => 0..L-1. good.
`vals += c` modifies vals; next eval `np.multiply` overwrites. Good.

In helper, initial a fill 0. Slices:
`a[m - i : m - i + n] += v` length n? m-i = n-1-i; end 2n-1-i. For i=n-1, start 0, end n. ok. For i=0, start n-1, end 2n-1. good.
`a[i : i + n] -= h_rev` end i+n; for i=n-1, end 2n-1. good.
For j similar. If h_rev is view reversed; `a[...] -= h_rev` aligns index t=0 with h[n-1]; as derived.

def pair_line_search_update(c,h,v,i,j,curmax,a,vals):
   n=h.size; m=n-1
   lo0 = max(-h[i], h[j]-1.0)
   hi0 = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
   if hi0 - lo0 < 1e-13: return curmax, 0.0
   # build a, q
   ...
   def? no nested.
   # Evaluate f0? f at 0 should curmax; maybe due drift.

   # endpoints
   def update candidate? Can't nested. We'll manually.
   f=pair_eval(... lo0); if f < f_best - eps: f_best=f; d_best=lo0
   f=pair_eval(... hi0); if f < f_best - eps: f_best=f; d_best=hi0
   # golden on safe interval if hi0-lo0 > 1e-8

      x1=hi - gr*(hi-lo); x2=lo + gr*(hi-lo)
      f1=pair_eval...; f2=...

      if f2 < f_best - eps: ...

         ...
         maybe update best after each new eval? Standard golden maintains f1/f2; optimum at one of x. At end evaluate midpoint.
   # after golden, if f_best < curmax - eps: apply

`random_binary_runs` implementation:
```
def random_binary_runs(rng,n):
    n=int(n)
    if n%2: n+=1
    total=n//2
    min_runs=2
    max_runs=max(3, min(total//3, n//6)) maybe
    R1=int(rng.integers(min_runs, max_runs+1))
    R0=int(rng.integers(min_runs, max_runs+1))
    # ensure |R1-R0|<=1
    if abs(R1-R0)>1:
       R1=(R1+R0)//2; R0=R1+(abs(R1-R0)%2?)
    start_one = R1 >= R0
    len1=random_composition(total,R1,rng)
    len0=random_composition(total,R0,rng)
    arr=[]; bit=1 if start_one else 0; b1=0;b0=0
    for _ in range(R1+R0):
       if bit==1:
          length=int(len1[b1]); b1+=1
          for _ in range(length): arr.append(1)
       else:
          length=int(len0[b0]); b0+=1
          for _ in range(length): arr.append(0)
       bit ^=1
    # length should n; if not, pad/trim and fix sum
    if len(arr)>n: arr=arr[:n]
    else: bit = 1 if (n-len(arr))%? pad with maybe current bit? better add zeros then fix
    b=np.array(arr,dtype=np.int8)
    while len(b)<n: b=np.concatenate([b,np.array([0],int8)])
    # fix sum: current ones count; need total. Flip pairs: if ones>total, change random ones to zero and zeros to one? If ones<total, change random zeros to one? This changes sum by pair.
```
Flipping pair to adjust count: If ones > total: need decrease ones by k. For each, find a 1 and a 0? But flipping both changes ones by 0 if one->0 and zero->1. To change count, need flip a single bit, which violates total half? We need total ones exactly n/2; if length fixed, count must be total. Since lengths sum total ones and zeros = n, count already total. If truncation changed, adjust by flipping one bit wrong direction and another? If missing ones, can flip a zero to one and a one to zero? That keeps count. To increase count by 1 impossible with length fixed without changing another. Need maintain count; if truncation removed some ones/zeros, count may be off by at most1. We can then flip one bit to fix (single) but total mean off by 0.5/n; later project? Implement:
```
def random_composition(total, parts, rng):
    parts = int(max(1, min(parts, total)))
    lengths = np.ones(parts, dtype=int)
    rem = total - parts
    if rem > 0:
       weights = np.ones(parts, dtype=float)
       adds = rng.multinomial(int(rem), weights / weights.sum())
       lengths += adds
    return lengths
```
parts could be 1. max_runs = max(3, min(max_runs, total//2?)) so parts <= total. Good.

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np

PAIR_GRID = 8
PAIR_GOLDEN = 18
PAIR_EPS = 1e-9


def project_sum_box(y, target):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    if y.size == 0:
        return y
    z = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(z.sum())
    diff = float(target) - s
    if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
        return z

    mask = (z > 1e-10) & (z < 1.0 - 1e-10)
    cnt = int(mask.sum())
    if cnt > 0:
        add = diff / float(cnt)
        zm = z[mask]
        if add > -float(zm.min()) + 1e-13 and add < 1.0 - float(zm.max()) - 1e-13:
            z[mask] += add
            z = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
            s2 = float(z.sum())
            if abs(s2 - target) < 1e-10:
                return z

    lo = float(y.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(y.max()) + 1.0
    if not (lo < hi):
        mid = (lo + hi) * 0.5
        z = np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        return z

    for _ in range(45):
        mid = (lo + hi) * 0.5
        val = float(np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if val > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    lam = (lo + hi) * 0.5
    z = np.clip(y - lam, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff2 = target - float(z.sum())
    if abs(diff2) > 1e-12:
        mask = (z > 1e-10) & (z < 1.0 - 1e-10)
        if mask.any():
            z[mask] += diff2 / float(mask.sum())
            z = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
    return z


def exact_c5(h):
    n = int(len(h))
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0
    dx = 2.0 / n
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(corr) * dx)


def evaluate_candidate(h):
    n = int(len(h))
    if n <= 0:
        return h.copy(), 0.0
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    h = project_sum_box(h, 0.5 * n)
    val = exact_c5(h)
    return h, val


def c_full_fft(h):
    n = int(len(h))
    m = n - 1
    L = 2 * n - 1
    M = 1
    while M < L:
        M <<= 1

    v = 1.0 - h
    vh = np.zeros(M)
    vh[:n] = v[::-1]

    fh = np.fft.rfft(h, n=M)
    fv = np.fft.rfft(vh, n=M)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fh * fv, n=M)[:L]
    return conv[::-1].copy()


def pair_eval(c, a, q0, q1, q2, d, vals):
    np.multiply(a, d, out=vals)
    vals += c
    if d != 0.0:
        dd = d * d
        vals[q0] -= 2.0 * dd
        if q1 >= 0:
            vals[q1] += dd
        if q2 >= 0:
            vals[q2] += dd
    return float(vals.max())


def pair_line_search_update(c, h, v, i, j, curmax, a, vals):
    n = h.size
    m = n - 1
    L = 2 * n - 1

    hi_i = float(h[i])
    lo_j = float(h[j])

    lo0 = max(-hi_i, lo_j - 1.0)
    hi0 = min(1.0 - hi_i, lo_j)
    if hi0 - lo0 < 1e-13:
        return curmax, 0.0

    a.fill(0.0)
    h_rev = h[::-1]

    a[m - i:m - i + n] += v
    a[i:i + n] -= h_rev

    a[m - j:m - j + n] -= v
    a[j:j + n] += h_rev

    q0 = m
    d12 = j - i
    q1 = m + d12
    q2 = m - d12

    eps = PAIR_EPS
    f_best = float(curmax)
    d_best = 0.0

    f = pair_eval(c, a, q0, q1, q2, lo0, vals)
    if f < f_best - eps:
        f_best = f
        d_best = lo0

    f = pair_eval(c, a, q0, q1, q2, hi0, vals)
    if f < f_best - eps:
        f_best = f
        d_best = hi0

    span = hi0 - lo0
    if span > 1e-12:
        g = PAIR_GRID
        for r in range(1, g):
            d = lo0 + span * (r / float(g))
            f = pair_eval(c, a, q0, q1, q2, d, vals)
            if f < f_best - eps:
                f_best = f
                d_best = d

        around = span / 6.0
        gl = max(lo0, d_best - around)
        gh = min(hi0, d_best + around)
        if gh - gl > 1e-9:
            phi = 0.6180339887498949
            x1 = gl + (1.0 - phi) * (gh - gl)
            x2 = gl + phi * (gh - gl)
            f1 = pair_eval(c, a, q0, q1, q2, x1, vals)
            if f1 < f_best - eps:
                f_best = f1
                d_best = x1
            f2 = pair_eval(c, a, q0, q1, q2, x2, vals)
            if f2 < f_best - eps:
                f_best = f2
                d_best = x2

            for _ in range(PAIR_GOLDEN):
                if gh - gl < 1e-10:
                    break
                if f1 < f2:
                    gh = x2
                    x2 = x1
                    f2 = f1
                    x1 = gl + (1.0 - phi) * (gh - gl)
                    f1 = pair_eval(c, a, q0, q1, q2, x1, vals)
                    if f1 < f_best - eps:
                        f_best = f1
                        d_best = x1
                else:
                    gl = x1
                    x1 = x2
                    f1 = f2
                    x2 = gl + phi * (gh - gl)
                    f2 = pair_eval(c, a, q0, q1, q2, x2, vals)
                    if f2 < f_best - eps:
                        f_best = f2
                        d_best = x2

            dmid = 0.5 * (gl + gh)
            f = pair_eval(c, a, q0, q1, q2, dmid, vals)
            if f < f_best - eps:
                f_best = f
                d_best = dmid

    if f_best < curmax - eps:
        d = min(max(d_best, lo0), hi0)
        dd = d * d

        np.multiply(a, d, out=vals)
        c += vals
        c[q0] -= 2.0 * dd
        if q1 >= 0:
            c[q1] += dd
        if q2 >= 0:
            c[q2] += dd

        newmax = float(c.max())
        if newmax < curmax - 0.5 * eps:
            return newmax, d

        c -= vals
        c[q0] += 2.0 * dd
        if q1 >= 0:
            c[q1] -= dd
        if q2 >= 0:
            c[q2] -= dd

    return curmax, 0.0


def perturb_h(h, target, scale, rng):
    y = h + scale * rng.normal(size=h.size)
    return project_sum_box(y, target)


def pair_refine(h, rng, deadline):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = h.size
    target = 0.5 * n
    L = 2 * n - 1
    m = n - 1

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(float(h.sum()) - target) > 1e-8:
        h = project_sum_box(h, target)

    c = c_full_fft(h)
    curmax = float(c.max())

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = curmax

    a = np.empty(L, dtype=float)
    vals = np.empty(L, dtype=float)
    v = 1.0 - h

    accepted_total = 0
    perturb_count = 0
    max_perturb = 16
    no_acc = 0

    full_mode = (n <= 180)

    while time.time() < deadline:
        if full_mode:
            improved = False
            for i in range(n):
                for j in range(i + 1, n):
                    if time.time() >= deadline:
                        return best_h
                    newmax, d = pair_line_search_update(c, h, v, i, j, curmax, a, vals)
                    if d != 0.0:
                        hi_val = h[i] + d
                        lo_val = h[j] - d
                        if hi_val < 0.0 and hi_val > -1e-8:
                            hi_val = 0.0
                        if hi_val > 1.0 and hi_val < 1.0 + 1e-8:
                            hi_val = 1.0
                        if lo_val < 0.0 and lo_val > -1e-8:
                            lo_val = 0.0
                        if lo_val > 1.0 and lo_val < 1.0 + 1e-8:
                            lo_val = 1.0
                        h[i] = hi_val
                        h[j] = lo_val
                        v = 1.0 - h
                        curmax = newmax
                        accepted_total += 1

                        if curmax < best_c - 1e-12:
                            best_c = curmax
                            best_h = h.copy()
                        improved = True
                        no_acc = 0

                        if (accepted_total % 80) == 0:
                            c = c_full_fft(h)
                            v = 1.0 - h
                            curmax = float(c.max())
                            if curmax < best_c - 1e-12:
                                best_c = curmax
                                best_h = h.copy()

            if not improved:
                if perturb_count < max_perturb:
                    scale = 0.035 + 0.012 * min(perturb_count, 8)
                    h = perturb_h(h, target, scale, rng)
                    c = c_full_fft(h)
                    v = 1.0 - h
                    curmax = float(c.max())
                    if curmax < best_c - 1e-12:
                        best_c = curmax
                        best_h = h.copy()
                    perturb_count += 1
                    no_acc = 0
                else:
                    break

        else:
            if no_acc > 1500:
                if perturb_count < max_perturb:
                    scale = 0.05 + 0.02 * min(perturb_count, 6)
                    h = perturb_h(h, target, scale, rng)
                    c = c_full_fft(h)
                    v = 1.0 - h
                    curmax = float(c.max())
                    if curmax < best_c - 1e-12:
                        best_c = curmax
                        best_h = h.copy()
                    perturb_count += 1
                    no_acc = 0
                else:
                    break
                continue

            K_lags = min(8, max(3, L // 40, 1))
            if K_lags > 0:
                top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -K_lags)[-K_lags:]
            else:
                top_idx = np.array([0], dtype=int)

            candidates = []
            seen = set()

            def_add_candidate = None  # no closure; add manually below

            for idx in top_idx:
                idx = int(idx)
                lag = idx - m
                if lag > 0:
                    Lg = lag
                    if Lg >= n:
                        continue
                    score = h[:n - Lg] * (1.0 - h[Lg:])
                    if score.size == 0:
                        continue
                    R = min(10, score.size)
                    if R <= 0:
                        continue
                    if score.size > R:
                        part = np.argpartition(score, -R)[-R:]
                    else:
                        part = np.argsort(score)[::-1][:R]
                    for p in part:
                        p = int(p)
                        if score[p] > 1e-7:
                            ii = p
                            jj = p + Lg
                            if ii != jj:
                                key = (ii, jj) if ii < jj else (jj, ii)
                                if key not in seen:
                                    seen.add(key)
                                    candidates.append((ii, jj))
                elif lag < 0:
                    Lg = -lag
                    if Lg >= n:
                        continue
                    score = h[Lg:] * (1.0 - h[:n - Lg])
                    if score.size == 0:
                        continue
                    R = min(10, score.size)
                    if R <= 0:
                        continue
                    if score.size > R:
                        part = np.argpartition(score, -R)[-R:]
                    else:
                        part = np.argsort(score)[::-1][:R]
                    for p in part:
                        p = int(p)
                        if score[p] > 1e-7:
                            ii = p + Lg
                            jj = p
                            if ii != jj:
                                key = (ii, jj) if ii < jj else (jj, ii)
                                if key not in seen:
                                    seen.add(key)
                                    candidates.append((ii, jj))

            rand_pairs = 40
            for _ in range(rand_pairs):
                ii = int(rng.integers(n))
                jj = int(rng.integers(n))
                if ii != jj:
                    key = (ii, jj) if ii < jj else (jj, ii)
                    if key not in seen:
                        seen.add(key)
                        candidates.append((ii, jj))

            max_cands = 260
            if len(candidates) > max_cands:
                # deterministic shuffle by index order is enough; preserve variability from candidates order
                candidates = candidates[:max_cands]

            accepted = False
            for ii, jj in candidates:
                if time.time() >= deadline:
                    break
                v = 1.0 - h
                newmax, d = pair_line_search_update(c, h, v, ii, jj, curmax, a, vals)
                if d != 0.0:
                    hi_val = h[ii] + d
                    lo_val = h[jj] - d
                    if hi_val < 0.0 and hi_val > -1e-8:
                        hi_val = 0.0
                    if hi_val > 1.0 and hi_val < 1.0 + 1e-8:
                        hi_val = 1.0
                    if lo_val < 0.0 and lo_val > -1e-8:
                        lo_val = 0.0
                    if lo_val > 1.0 and lo_val < 1.0 + 1e-8:
                        lo_val = 1.0
                    h[ii] = hi_val
                    h[jj] = lo_val
                    curmax = newmax
                    accepted_total += 1
                    no_acc = 0

                    if curmax < best_c - 1e-12:
                        best_c = curmax
                        best_h = h.copy()

                    if (accepted_total % 40) == 0:
                        c = c_full_fft(h)
                        curmax = float(c.max())
                        if curmax < best_c - 1e-12:
                            best_c = curmax
                            best_h = h.copy()

                    accepted = True
                    break
                else:
                    no_acc += 1

            if not accepted:
                no_acc += 1


    return best_h


def round_to_binary(h, rng=None):
    n = len(h)
    k = n // 2
    if rng is None:
        order = np.argsort(-h, kind="mergesort")
    else:
        order = np.argsort(-(h + 1e-9 * rng.random(n)), kind="mergesort")
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    b[order[:k]] = 1
    return b


def binary_fill_delta(hbits, vbits, hrev, delta, i, j, m, n):
    delta.fill(0)
    delta[m - i:m - i + n] -= vbits
    delta[i:i + n] += hrev
    delta[m - j:m - j + n] += vbits
    delta[j:j + n] -= hrev
    Ldiff = j - i
    if Ldiff != 0:
        idx1 = m + Ldiff
        idx2 = m - Ldiff
        delta[idx1] += 1
        delta[idx2] += 1


def binary_refine(hbits, c_int, curmax, rng, deadline):
    hbits = np.asarray(hbits, dtype=np.int8).copy()
    c_int = np.asarray(c_int, dtype=np.int64).copy()
    curmax = int(c_int.max())
    n = hbits.size
    m = n - 1
    L = 2 * n - 1

    best_h = hbits.copy()
    best_max = curmax

    vbits = 1 - hbits
    hrev = hbits[::-1]
    delta = np.empty(L, dtype=np.int64)

    start = time.time()
    total = max(1.0, max(0.0, deadline - start))
    maxT = max(1.0, float(n) / 20.0)

    no_imp = 0
    attempts = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        frac = max(0.0, (deadline - time.time()) / total)
        T = maxT * frac * frac

        top = int(np.argmax(c_int))
        lag = top - m
        target_cands = []

        B = 12
        if lag > 0:
            Lg = lag
            if Lg < n:
                cand = np.nonzero((hbits[:n - Lg] == 1) & (hbits[Lg:] == 0))[0]
                if cand.size:
                    if cand.size > B:
                        pick = rng.choice(cand, size=B, replace=False)
                    else:
                        pick = cand
                    for p in pick:
                        ii = int(p)
                        jj = int(p + Lg)
                        if 0 <= ii < n and 0 <= jj < n and ii != jj:
                            target_cands.append((ii, jj))
        elif lag < 0:
            Lg = -lag
            if Lg < n:
                cand = np.nonzero((hbits[Lg:] == 1) & (hbits[:n - Lg] == 0))[0]
                if cand.size:
                    if cand.size > B:
                        pick = rng.choice(cand, size=B, replace=False)
                    else:
                        pick = cand
                    for p in pick:
                        ii = int(p + Lg)
                        jj = int(p)
                        if 0 <= ii < n and 0 <= jj < n and ii != jj:
                            target_cands.append((ii, jj))

        trial_pairs = list(target_cands)

        Rrand = 8
        for _ in range(Rrand):
            while True:
                ii = int(rng.integers(n))
                if hbits[ii] == 1:
                    break
            while True:
                jj = int(rng.integers(n))
                if hbits[jj] == 0:
                    break
            if ii != jj:
                trial_pairs.append((ii, jj))

        moved = False
        for ii, jj in trial_pairs:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            if hbits[ii] != 1 or hbits[jj] != 0:
                continue

            binary_fill_delta(hbits, vbits, hrev, delta, ii, jj, m, n)
            c_int += delta
            newmax = int(c_int.max())
            diff = newmax - curmax

            accept = False
            if diff < 0:
                accept = True
            elif diff == 0:
                if rng.random() < 0.15:
                    accept = True
            elif T > 1e-8 and diff < 60.0 * T:
                if rng.random() < math.exp(-float(diff) / max(1e-12, T)):
                    accept = True

            if accept:
                hbits[ii], hbits[jj] = hbits[jj], hbits[ii]
                vbits = 1 - hbits
                hrev = hbits[::-1]
                curmax = newmax
                moved = True
                no_imp = 0

                if curmax < best_max:
                    best_max = curmax
                    best_h = hbits.copy()
                break
            else:
                c_int -= delta
                no_imp += 1
                attempts += 1

        if not moved:
            no_imp += 1
            attempts += 1

        if T < 0.4 and no_imp > 20000:
            break

    return best_h


def random_composition(total, parts, rng):
    total = int(total)
    parts = int(max(1, min(parts, total)))
    lengths = np.ones(parts, dtype=int)
    rem = total - parts
    if rem > 0:
        adds = rng.multinomial(rem, np.full(parts, 1.0 / float(parts)))
        lengths += adds
    return lengths


def random_binary_runs(rng, n):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2:
        n += 1
    total = n // 2

    min_runs = 3
    max_runs = max(min_runs + 1, min(n // 8, total // 2))
    R1 = int(rng.integers(min_runs, max_runs + 1))
    R0 = int(rng.integers(min_runs, max_runs + 1))
    if abs(R1 - R0) > 1:
        Ravg = (R1 + R0) // 2
        R1 = Ravg
        R0 = Ravg + (abs(R1 - R0) % 2)

    len1 = random_composition(total, R1, rng)
    len0 = random_composition(total, R0, rng)

    start_one = (R1 >= R0)
    bit = 1 if start_one else 0
    arr = []
    b1 = 0
    b0 = 0

    for _ in range(R1 + R0):
        if bit == 1:
            for _ in range(int(len1[b1])):
                arr.append(1)
            b1 += 1
        else:
            for _ in range(int(len0[b0])):
                arr.append(0)
            b0 += 1
        bit ^= 1

    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    w = min(n, len(arr))
    out[:w] = np.array(arr[:w], dtype=np.int8)

    ones = int(out.sum())
    if ones < total:
        idx = np.nonzero(out == 0)[0]
        if idx.size:
            choose = idx[:total - ones]
            out[choose] = 1
    elif ones > total:
        idx = np.nonzero(out == 1)[0]
        if idx.size:
            choose = idx[:ones - total]
            out[choose] = 0

    ones = int(out.sum())
    if ones < total:
        idx = np.nonzero(out == 0)[0]
        if idx.size:
            choose = idx[(total - ones):] if len(idx) >= (total - ones) else idx
            out[:total - ones] = 1
    elif ones > total:
        idx = np.nonzero(out == 1)[0]
        if idx.size:
            out[idx[:ones - total]] = 0

    return out


def upsample_to(h_old, n_new):
    n_old = len(h_old)
    n_new = int(n_new)
    if n_new % 2:
        n_new += 1
    if n_old == n_new:
        return np.asarray(h_old, dtype=float).copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new, endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, np.asarray(h_old, dtype=float))


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s)
    work_dead = deadline - max(25.0, 0.04 * float(budget_s))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent_obj = globals().get("initial_h_values", None)
    if parent_obj is not None:
        parent_h = np.asarray(parent_obj, dtype=float).copy()
    else:
        parent_h = random_binary_runs(rng, 180).astype(float)

    if len(parent_h) <= 0:
        parent_h = random_binary_runs(rng, 180).astype(float)

    if len(parent_h) % 2:
        parent_h = upsample_to(parent_h, len(parent_h) + 1)

    parent_h, _ = evaluate_candidate(parent_h)
    best_h = parent_h.copy()
    best_val = _

    base_n = len(best_h)

    # Stage 1: strong local pair search at the parent resolution.
    stage_deadline = min(work_dead, time.time() + 0.42 * float(budget_s))
    h_cand = pair_refine(best_h.copy(), rng, stage_deadline)
    h_cand, val = evaluate_candidate(h_cand)
    if val < best_val:
        best_val = val
        best_h = h_cand

    # Stage 2: binary rounding + binary swap annealing at parent/best resolution.
    if time.time() < work_dead:
        stage_deadline = min(work_dead, time.time() + 0.12 * float(budget_s))
        b = round_to_binary(best_h, rng)
        ct = np.rint(c_full_fft(b.astype(float))).astype(np.int64)
        b2 = binary_refine(b, ct, int(ct.max()), rng, stage_deadline)
        h_cand, val = evaluate_candidate(b2.astype(float))
        if val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_h = h_cand

    # Stage 3: upscale to 2x and run pair local search.
    n2 = 2 * base_n
    if n2 >= 60 and n2 <= 2048:
        stage_deadline = min(work_dead, time.time() + 0.20 * float(budget_s))
        h_up = upsample_to(best_h, n2)
        h_up = project_sum_box(np.clip(h_up, 0.0, 1.0), 0.5 * len(h_up))
        h_cand = pair_refine(h_up, rng, stage_deadline)
        h_cand, val = evaluate_candidate(h_cand)
        if val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_h = h_cand

    # Stage 4: upscale toward 4x and run pair local search.
    n4 = 4 * base_n
    if n4 >= 120 and n4 <= 4096 and n4 > n2:
        stage_deadline = min(work_dead, time.time() + 0.15 * float(budget_s))
        h_up = upsample_to(best_h, n4)
        h_up = project_sum_box(np.clip(h_up, 0.0, 1.0), 0.5 * len(h_up))
        h_cand = pair_refine(h_up, rng, stage_deadline)
        h_cand, val = evaluate_candidate(h_cand)
        if val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_h = h_cand

    # Stage 5: if useful time remains, polish current best one more time.
    if time.time() < work_dead - 20:
        stage_deadline = min(work_dead, time.time() + 0.06 * float(budget_s))
        h_cand = pair_refine(best_h.copy(), rng, stage_deadline)
        h_cand, val = evaluate_candidate(h_cand)
        if val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_h = h_cand

    # Optional final random binary attempt if there is still meaningful time.
    if time.time() < work_dead - 35:
        n_rand = min(4 * base_n, 2048)
        if n_rand < 60:
            n_rand = max(n_rand, 180)
        b = random_binary_runs(rng, n_rand)
        ct = np.rint(c_full_fft(b.astype(float))).astype(np.int64)
        b2 = binary_refine(b, ct, int(ct.max()), rng, work_dead)
        h_cand, val = evaluate_candidate(b2.astype(float))
        if val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_h = h_cand

    # Final feasibility and exact objective.
    best_h, best_val = evaluate_candidate(best_h)
    return (best_h, best_val, len(best_h))
```
